[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where its citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

[Merchants Run]
Smoke steadily rises from the chimneys of this massive stone building on the north side of the well maintained road. Through the open facade young Vorngar can be seen pumping the bellows while the master heats a rod of glowing iron within the forge.  The clanking sound of metal on metal can be heard as the gifted apprentices create and sharpen weapons of all types. The hot fires of the forge radiate all around. A large sign mounted above a tall studded iron door clearly reads, "Talafon's Weapons and Armor".  On the south side of the road a small colorful building reflects light cast from two glowing orbs mounted on each side of a glittering portal floating between a silver archway. Above the archway a small but functional sign reads "Malinar's Magic and Mayham".

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where its citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

[Cheery Boardwalk]
Bright strings of enchanted glowing crystals are strung around every pole, tree, roof, and fence. The brisk air ebbs slowly to and fro, causing delicate snow crystals to wisp against the clothes and skin of passersby. A polished teak boardwalk leads from behind the branches of the massive evergreen towards a row of quaint cottages. Tall lamp posts, housing glowing ice sculptures of avatars of the Gods line the walkway on the opposite side, tethered by garlands of pine and mistletoe.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: north, icy passage between the trees, frosted crystal door

[Cheery Boardwalk]
Snow banks at the side of the boardwalk have been packed together where the accumulated precipitation has been pushed to keep the walkway clear. A few patrons exit from a cottage sporting  new hats and coats.

The area is illuminated.

[Cheery Boardwalk]

[Cheery Boardwalk]

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where its citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where its citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

[Traders Clearing]
Wagon tracks run through the rocky road, ending just before a row of wooden cabins. Signs hang over the door of the three largest, revealing that they house various shops catering to both the residents of the estate and the many travelers that pass through. The sound of hammer meeting anvil come from the nearest, steel door, while bright lights dance behind the window of the closer wooden door. A simple sign above the far oaken door suggests that it leads to a bank. A display window beside the bank reveals that even a clothing shop is present.

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where its citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

[Double Boil]
Stone walls glow dimly by torchlight. The adamant glow of a large black cauldron churns with anticipation from the stoking hot red coals beneath it. Mason jars, filled with ingredients ranging from spider legs to ghoul spit, sit atop long oak shelves mounted on the eastern and western walls. Steam flows contently out of the bubbling water, casting a strange scent muddled by the stale air of the shop. A large, heavy wooden door creaks as it opens and closes in unison with the comings and goings of patrons who pass through.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: door

go door

[Merchants Run]
The neatly manicured stone street bustles with activity as the sounds of market resonate excitedly. Travelers, merchants and city residents gather in a foray of discussion over their latest purchases, as the varying smells of cured leathers, hot metal, and baked spices mingle overhead in the air. A small shop constructed in black shingles attempts to blend into the shadows on the southern side of the street, its large wooden door beckoning patrons to enter. A small wooden sign posted above the door reads "Double Boil."

The area is illuminated.

[Merchants Run]
Smoke steadily rises from the chimneys of this massive stone building on the north side of the well maintained road. Through the open facade young Vorngar can be seen pumping the bellows while the master heats a rod of glowing iron within the forge.  The clanking sound of metal on metal can be heard as the gifted apprentices create and sharpen weapons of all types. The hot fires of the forge radiate all around. A large sign mounted above a tall studded iron door clearly reads, "Talafon's Weapons and Armor".  On the south side of the road a small colorful building reflects light cast from two glowing orbs mounted on each side of a glittering portal floating between a silver archway. [The Soles of Urda]
Towering shelves of oak line the walls, row upon row of expertly crafted footwear have been set out on display. A diminutive Fae flutters around the shop, organizing the displays, keeping the shoes polished, and greeting people as customers come and go in the busy store. A callous-fingered Silvarin sits toward the back of the shop, her dexterous hands moving in a blur as she finishes crafting a mate to the completed boot already sitting on the table beside her. With a deep sigh, she sets the completed shoe on the table, waiting for her assistant to clean them and set them out for sale.

[Southern Cross Road]
Cobblestone streets void of dirt and litter extend north and south. Sounds of chatter and laughter can be heard clearly to the north and muffled sounds of horse hooves and creaky wagon wheels to the south. The occasional city waste engineer passes by to check the large black iron trashcan siting on the eastern side of the road.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: south, north


[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where its citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where its citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. [Traders Clearing]
Wagon tracks run through the rocky road, ending just before a row of wooden cabins. Signs hang over the door of the three largest, revealing that they house various shops catering to both the residents of the estate and the many travelers that pass through. The sound of hammer meeting anvil come from the nearest, steel door, while bright lights dance behind the window of the closer wooden door. A simple sign above the far oaken door suggests that it leads to a bank. A display window beside the bank reveals that even a clothing shop is present.

The area is illuminated.

The area is illuminated.

Trees have grown into a natural wall, protecting the clearing from any unwanted visitors. Vines have been woven through the tangled branches of the trees, blocking the rest of the forest from view. Soft grass peppered with patches of clover grows underfoot, and a small, brown rabbit nibbles contentedly on the green stalks.  A small group of sturdy Galirrim has gathered around a small spring of crystal clear water. Enchanting scents of cooking food waft through the air from a painted-wood tavern, closely followed by the sound merry laughter.

The area is illuminated.

[Traders Clearing]
Wagon tracks run through the rocky road, ending just before a row of wooden cabins. Signs hang over the door of the three largest, revealing that they house various shops catering to both the residents of the estate and the many travelers that pass through. The sound of hammer meeting anvil come from the nearest, steel door, while bright lights dance behind the window of the closer wooden door. A simple sign above the far oaken door suggests that it leads to a bank. A display window beside the bank reveals that even a clothing shop is present.

The area is illuminated.
